## `AFG-1313-OPS-R1-OPS-SC-01`

- **Country:** `AFG` · **Track:** `OPS`
- **Role:** Dyeing Operator
- **Scenario:** Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Textile Stenter Setting Machine & High-Temperature Overflow Dyeing
- **Intro:** Dyeing Operator — Pre-Shift Cold Check, Baseline Parameter Calibration & Commissioning SOP for Textile Stenter Setting Machine & High-Temperature Overflow Dyeing — Lockout/Tagout (LOTO) Verification and Electrical Isolation: Voltage reading must be 0 V AC (no residual voltage above 0.5 V).
